# Foundation Wikipedia — Ralph Bridge write and CI incident evidence (2026-10-15)

## Scope and interpretation

This is an evidence record for the current recovery session, not a claim that every previous failure was caused by Ralph Bridge. Entries distinguish observable facts from hypotheses and never include credentials, authorization tokens or raw user data. Canonical implementation TODO is not edited.

## Identity and preflight

- Repository: `ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia`, branch: `master` (the only inventoried branch; no associated open PRs).
- Ralph Bridge ping: success; protocol schema 3, semantic version `0.6.2`, build `0acea4b23362e2ccecab8f6c9db4418b162426cf`, server instance `51c945dd809b4207800c3ba33151ad94`, started `2026-10-15T02:25:51.132200Z`, auth mode `app`.
- Pre-repair exact master head: `5f4db5db8ce2c918785a9744dc1ae5fe8c7bd766`.
- Canonical `docs/WIKIPEDIA_AI_READER_TODO.md`: `total_lines=1536`, original blob SHA `ec594ed49cf4fff5e1c7ab42fca927881be55df5`. The complete TODO was inspected through eight consecutive 200-line-or-shorter windows; do not infer completeness from a truncated response.
- `docs/SRC_003_CENSUS_PIPELINE_2026-10-14.md`: `total_lines=346`, blob `0191d49981243930fce5039d03225248068ac51d`; preserved.

## Incident 1: failing exact-head Rust formatting (confirmed)

- Original master SHA `5f4db5db8ce2c918785a9744dc1ae5fe8c7bd766`.
- Rust workspace workflow [run 38707257176](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38707257176), job `116329632286`, step `Format`: failed with exit code 1. Evidence lines 302–341 show two `rustfmt` line-wrap differences in `crates/wiki-source/src/public_dump_revision_jsonl.rs` (test at approximately line 325/341).
- Platform shells [run 38707257159](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38707257159): succeeded on the same SHA.
- Repair: Ralph Bridge `create_or_update_file`, expected head `5f4db5db8ce2c918785a9744dc1ae5fe8c7bd766`, exact path `crates/wiki-source/src/public_dump_revision_jsonl.rs`, only two formatting substitutions, UTF-8 content length `15107` bytes. Operation ID `dc71328cea6c4edd9ccbeb1d316a24e8`, request time `2026-10-15T10:50:05.727537Z`; returned `accepted=true` and `write_reconciled=true`.
- **Important warning**: `GitHub ref-update acknowledgement was uncertain; Ralph re-read the branch and confirmed the intended commit as its head.` Two further warnings confirmed exact parent/diff verification and that CI must be checked separately. New verified head: `fcbd4ed0cf815013bba8f1368b20be22c830a70c`, parent `5f4db5db8ce2c918785a9744dc1ae5fe8c7bd766`. **Do not blindly retry ambiguous writes.**
- Recovery exact-head qualification: [Rust 38723062099](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38723062099) and [Platform 38723062150](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38723062150), both successful. Canonical TODO remains unchanged.

## Incident 2: earlier write rejections (incomplete evidence)

- Previous run reported rejected write operations against `master`, including the formatting repair and an attempted restoration, before execution. The actual tool response status/error payload, operation IDs, input diagnostics and timestamps were not retained in this session. Therefore **the rejecting layer is unconfirmed**; do not attribute the failures definitively to bridge, GitHub or a platform safety gate.
- The repair succeeded on the first observed write attempt of this session. This is a successful retry on a newer bridge process, **not** proof that the underlying intermittent issue was resolved.

## Incident 3: unrelated intermittent CI subprocess failures (historical evidence)

- Historical Rust CI attempts sometimes reported `online_curl` tests returning `SpawnFailed` where `ResponseTooLarge` or an HTTP status was expected; documented in `docs/SRC_003_CENSUS_PIPELINE_2026-10-14.md` (for example run 38666642949 and first attempt of 38667126349). Later retries passed without changing curl transport code.
- `crates/wiki-source/src/online_curl.rs` previously collapsed all `std::process::Command::spawn` errors to the single `CurlTransportError::SpawnFailed` marker. The actual `ErrorKind` and raw OS errno were unavailable in test logs.
- The instrumentation added in the accompanying source change emits only `ErrorKind` and `raw_os_error` to stderr on a failed spawn, without exposing request URLs, executable paths, environment variables or tokens. A deterministic non-executable-path test protects the unchanged public `SpawnFailed` API contract. These diagnostics can distinguish `EAGAIN` / process-resource limits from `EACCES` / permission denial, `ENOENT` / missing binaries and `ETXTBSY` / executable contention on later failures. This instrumentation is **not** a fix for the underlying flake.

## Future incident capture checklist

For any future write or test failure, preserve: UTC timestamp; bridge version/build/instance/start/auth mode; operation/tool name; sanitized argument shape and paths (not credentials or full source content); branch/exact expected SHA and observed post-failure SHA; returned operation ID, accepted/reconciled status, warning/error type, message and structured diagnostics; CI run/job/step ID, run attempt, exact SHA and bounded log window. Re-read branch state before any retry of an ambiguous mutation. Record whether the failure happened before tool invocation, within bridge validation, after GitHub ref-update acknowledgment, or during CI; if the layer cannot be shown, mark it unknown. Follow source integrity and exact-head CI checks after recovery.

## Outstanding limitations

Full SRC-002/SRC-003 source authenticity, production Rust XML/bzip2 parsing, independent coverage and rendered/offline media acceptance remain open. This document is troubleshooting evidence, not completion of those TODOs.

- Initial implementation SHA: `a354b18ff0dcd47ddd7cc912b95a1a6f8167fbed`; Ralph Bridge `commit_files` operation ID `89cafc347f174641b590b1d891c8d068`, accepted and exact-head verified at `2026-10-15T11:01:00.633641Z`.

A doubled escape in an expected byte newline was also corrected proactively.
- Repair: `create_or_update_file` operation ID `8c660969d8b64c5aad002308bc02544b`, accepted, from exact head `a354b18ff0dcd47ddd7cc912b95a1a6f8167fbed` to `f01c32a7a92d23d9b7aa18b43a3bb57d5949a46d`. No ambiguity warning was reported.

Useful pattern: a remote source commit can pass bridge acceptance and still fail Python discovery; commits must be separately tested on exact-head CI.

## Incident 5: read-only orchestration timeout (underlying layer unknown)

- Occurred after documentation commit `da95d7f3d506657ac60ec589fd5b9d4cfc12d4f7` and before successful recheck at `2026-10-15T11:12:50.077235Z`. Exact call-start and timeout time were not returned by the orchestration layer.
- A single `functions.exec` invocation attempted **sequential** Ralph Bridge reads: `get_repo_state(master)`, `get_file` of the canonical TODO at the exact head, `get_file` of the SRC-003 census document at the exact head, and `get_ci_outcome` on that exact head. The host returned exactly `Script error: Code Mode execution timed out.` No nested call result was delivered, so it is **unknown** which sub-call was in flight at expiration. **There was no write call in this request.** This wrapper error did not include a bridge operation ID, HTTP status, stack trace, or nested response payload.
- Recovery isolated smaller read requests, without modifying or restarting the bridge. `ralph_ping` returned `pong`, `get_repo_state(master)` returned the unchanged exact head `da95d7f3d506657ac60ec589fd5b9d4cfc12d4f7` at `2026-10-15T11:12:50.077235Z`; `get_ci_outcome` reported success on [Rust 38725016060](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38725016060) and [Platform 38725016126](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38725016126). Individual integrity reads confirmed the unchanged TODO blob SHA `ec594ed49cf4fff5e1c7ab42fca927881be55df5` (1,536 lines) and SRC-003 census blob `0191d49981243930fce5039d03225248068ac51d` (346 lines).
- Subsequent `get_runtime_info` still reported Ralph Bridge `0.6.2`, build `0acea4b23362e2ccecab8f6c9db4418b162426cf`, process start `2026-10-15T02:25:51.132200Z`, instance `51c945dd809b4207800c3ba33151ad94`, auth mode `app`. There was **no observed bridge process restart**.
- **Classification:** an unlocalized orchestration/read timeout followed by read recovery. This is not evidence of a failed repository mutation, a GitHub outage or a specific Ralph Bridge bug. To localize a recurrence, make one Ralph read per orchestrator call and preserve per-call entry/return diagnostics, while still respecting source integrity and exact-head constraints.

## Incident 6: LF-only regression fixture used a literal escape (confirmed local reproduction; remote CI pending)

- **Source baseline:** the user-supplied GitHub `master` archive dated 2026-10-15 contains commit `4191078de2b4f1a71462e82eeb11521ed718bc3b` and the original 1,536-line canonical TODO (blob `ec594ed49cf4fff5e1c7ab42fca927881be55df5`). `docs/SRC_003_CENSUS_PIPELINE_2026-10-14.md` retains 346 lines (blob `0191d49981243930fce5039d03225248068ac51d`). The snapshot is **not** a live post-patch GitHub verification.
- **Prior CI failure reported in the recovery session:** Rust workspace [run 38728303849](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38728303849), job `116399502112`, step `Verify fixture provenance`. The original first-party report described a test-fixture issue; exact raw GitHub log lines were not captured through the unavailable Ralph Bridge in this patch-preparation session.
- **Deterministic local reproduction:** `python3 -m unittest discover -s scripts -p 'test_audit_public_dump_revision_jsonl.py' -v` on the unmodified archive (Python 3.13) ran six methods but failed both `content=False` and `content=True` subcases of `test_generation_cutoff_and_lf_contract_for_both_revision_modes`. The fixture inserted the *literal* bytes backslash and `r` (`b",\\r "` in Python source) into a JSON object, causing `RevisionAuditError: invalid diagnostic JSON record`; it expected an `unterminated`-record error caused by an actual carriage return.
- **Local correction:** replace the malformed fixture with `raw.replace(b", ", b"," + bytes([13]) + b" ", 1)` and assert the injected bytes actually include `bytes([13])`. This exercises the existing LF-only production decoder behavior without changing it. Test inputs/outputs are synthetic and no secrets are involved.
- **Local post-patch checks:** six targeted JSONL-audit tests passed (Python 3.13), plus 21 source-staging tests, 31 XML-census tests, fixture-provenance verification and Python compilation checks. Full Python discovery ran 302 tests with **one environment-only error**: the dependency-license test requires `cargo metadata`, but Cargo is not installed in this test sandbox. Remote exact-head Rust/Platform CI remains unverified.
- **Boundary:** the patch was produced from the supplied archive and must be applied and CI-qualified against the observed live GitHub `master` head. It does not establish full public-dump source authentication, a portable Rust XML importer, independent source completeness, rendering fidelity, or mandatory offline-media acceptance. Do not mark SRC-003 complete based on this test repair.
- **Root cause classification:** authored regression fixture defect, not evidence of a Ralph Bridge implementation bug. A separate prior Ralph Bridge tunnel-client no-poll response and post-restart internal errors blocked the remote recovery; those should be diagnosed independently, without automatically attributing this test failure to the bridge.

## 2026-10-15 — Renderer dump qualification failed on unsupported sanitized-css (not a bridge error)

- Exact failed head: `4a1a7926c9523e16a15ea22ebce8de6b10d0ac84`; [renderer dump run 38801394479](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38801394479), job `116651042546`, step 5, import into pinned MediaWiki. GitHub logged `MWUnknownContentModelException: The content model 'sanitized-css' is not registered on this wiki` immediately after importing approximately 2,000 pages. Failure log window 267–306. Stage discovery and SHA-256 verification passed. Three other exact-head workflows passed (Rust `38801394383`, Platform `38801394406`, synthetic renderer fixtures `38801394455`).
- Confirmed cause: the public-dump runner used pinned MediaWiki 1.43.11 without TemplateStyles, which registers the sanitized-css content model used by real Wikipedia pages. No source pages should be silently dropped to conceal this failure.
- Remedy under qualification: keep the existing synthetic fixture runner on its independently qualified 1.43 image, and use a separate immutable MediaWiki 1.44 image-index digest for the public-dump runner (`sha256:7bd97e0a6dd7d662e0cab93bb6a2e51b25c6a1d528f4e5ba88dc529dacf2224a`). Require bundled TemplateStyles on disk, load the extension and check API-reported version/extension registration **before** importing. Preserve image and extension provenance in the existing output artifacts.
- Bridge health in the recovery session: `ralph_ping=pong`; v0.6.2 build `0acea4b23362e2ccecab8f6c9db4418b162426cf`, instance `1ebac85d2b264606b870b73b4177ba47`, started `2026-10-15T20:35:26.505712Z`, GitHub App authentication. Reads and failure/job-log retrieval succeeded. These facts do **not** attribute the renderer CI failure to Ralph Bridge.
- Local qualification: `bash -n tools/renderer-harness/run_dump.sh` succeeded and 12 Python unit tests for the dump filter/render comparator passed against the supplied exact-head ZIP. Container-based public dump import requires GitHub Actions CI; until it passes, SRC-003 stays unchecked.

## 2026-10-15 — Rust CI proves ETXTBSY subprocess-spawn flake; bounded remediation

- At exact head `6ed7aea0f1e0561e74f912bd071c0c0da0f337d7`, Rust workspace [38808470226](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38808470226), job `116675350254`, step 6 `Test`, failed in `online_curl::tests::caps_streamed_body_without_trusting_content_length`. Focused CI log lines 1192–1231 show **`wiki-source curl spawn failed: kind=ExecutableFileBusy, raw_os_error=Some(26)`**, followed by `Err(SpawnFailed)` instead of expected `Err(ResponseTooLarge)`; 189 other tests passed. This is Linux `ETXTBSY`, not an upstream GitHub/bridge error. The earlier safe errno instrumentation made this previously opaque flake diagnosable.
- At this same code SHA, [Platform shells 38808470243](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38808470243) and [Renderer dump 38808470283](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38808470283) passed.
- Remedy: on **failed executable spawn only**, retry `std::io::ErrorKind::ExecutableFileBusy` at most three times with bounded 20/40/60 ms backoff. No process has been created when spawn itself returns `ETXTBSY`, so this does not duplicate requests. Preserve existing `SpawnFailed` behavior for nonretriable errors, including permission denial. Deterministic Linux regression tests inject two transient failures, four persistent failures, and a nonretriable permission denial to prove the retry boundary.
- This is a CI subprocess race fix, independent of SRC-003 public dump authenticity and renderer correctness. Exact-head CI is required before claiming qualification; no new branch or PR.

## 2026-10-15 — Concurrent master writes: exact-head reconciliation

- A separate session moved `master` from `0be02a3ddde2de2092dc32dd81c68e755270358c` to `1c97ce8678766e36377d578301ea577f2f032a89` while this run prepared source-ID reporting. Ralph Bridge `get_repo_state(master)` caught the changed SHA **before** any write was attempted, avoiding a stale-head mutation.
- The concurrent `1c97ce` commit correctly separated importer-created local page/revision IDs from original Wikimedia IDs but also accidentally introduced two trailing backslashes (ASCII 92,92) in Python test continuation statements. Renderer dump run `38810887330` failed during step 3 `Unit tests` with a Python syntax error at line 72. The other session repaired these two lines at `7d7b9ebcc92d9071c1eb5b1dc6d442cd73aa77d3`, and all three exact-head workflows passed: [Rust 38810972947](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38810972947), [Platform 38810972897](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38810972897), [Renderer dump 38810972952](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38810972952).
- Before the second attempted reconciliation, another preflight detected `master` moving from `1c97ce` to `7d7b9e`; no stale write was attempted. A fresh inspection of `7d7b9e` confirmed the other session's syntax fix. Work was **not duplicated**. All operations used Ralph Bridge; no branch or PR was created.
- The source-to-local audit records 25/25 remapped local page/revision IDs but 25/25 matching source revision timestamps. This is a mapping requirement, **not proof of content corruption**. The narrow follow-up adds `source_page_id` to the per-page record beside the existing original revision ID and local IDs. Authenticating the source content bytes and mapping them to original citations remains necessary.
- **Concurrency pattern:** multiple active master-writing sessions can cause stale expected-head preconditions, duplicated task selection and transient unqualified commits. Compare-and-swap prevented conflicts, but a single active writer per repo is preferable to reduce wasted CI and repeated edits. No Ralph Bridge transport failure occurred in these observations.

## 2026-10-15 — Staged media namespace fix: rustfmt-only CI failure and correction

- **Confirmed source/CI failure**, not a Ralph Bridge or GitHub/API failure. No ambiguous acknowledgment.

## 2026-10-15 — Read pagination guard corrected (caller-side; no GitHub mutation)

- While preparing the rustfmt-only repair against exact master `22b20e86ca7fb6ba06bf1fe569c54cdec5d83ba2`, a read/assembly orchestrator script incorrectly assumed its bounded `get_file` response for `crates/wiki-pack-builder/src/media.rs` was complete. The source has **328 lines**; the first read returned only lines **1–300**, explicitly `next_start_line=301`. The caller raised `Error: unexpected incomplete file` **before** any `commit_files` or other mutation. No GitHub acceptance receipt was expected or emitted for this local guard failure.
- Recovery: inspected metadata and read explicit 1–300 and 301–328 windows, verified both windows' matching blob SHA and `next_start_line=null` at the last window, then performed the intended CAS-guarded formatting-and-incident commit. Ralph operation `eeb93ec29d7e4ccda4a683099043ed1f` returned `accepted=true`, `write_reconciled=false`, observed head `8e72cfcd3c9825c611abc44f34154bb3803d9da0`.
- **Classification:** local read-orchestration precondition error. There is no evidence that Ralph Bridge, GitHub, authorization, or source tests failed in this pre-write event. Mitigation: inspect `next_start_line` and `total_lines` for every bounded read; do not accept a single returned content window as a full file.
- The first bounded `wait_for_ci_outcome` on diagnostics commit `14b3cd0f3d01c68d1e082dd8d2c37f7526ee9aef` exhausted its explicit **55-second** budget after **11 polls** and **zero** rate-limit events with state `pending`. Subsequent exact-head reads verified all three workflows succeeded (Rust `38818954184`, Platform `38818954128`, Renderer `38818954145`). **Classification:** ordinary bounded wait expiration, not a proven CI/GitHub/Bridge failure.
- Latest documentation qualification head `e98228c41726ead90f800fb1cbeceebade395eeb` passed [Rust CI 38820033160](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38820033160) and [Platform CI 38820033167](https://github.com/ekkus93/foundation-wikipedia/actions/runs/38820033167).
